===== FILE backend/app/doubao_endpoints.py =====
"""豆包推理接入点登记、类目工作流绑定与运行时解析。

合同要点：
- 每个类目工作流同一时间只绑定一个接入点；一个接入点可被多个同类目工作流共用（存量回归常有多条工作流并行）。接入点 ID 是成本归因主键。
- 接入点在火山控制台创建；平台只做录入、格式校验、连通性验收、绑定、停用。
- 接入点路由开关打开后，使用豆包 2.0 的工作流缺少接入点即禁止发起真实调用。
- 运行首次真实调用时把接入点冻结到运行上；改绑只影响之后创建的运行。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import select, or_
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import append_audit_event
from .doubao_endpoint_models import DoubaoInferenceEndpoint, VolcengineBillingSettings
from .model_pricing import is_volcengine_ark_provider
from .models import EvaluationCategoryProfile, ModelRegistryEntry, ProductionRun, WorkflowDefinition

ENDPOINT_ID_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^ep-(?:bi-)?[0-9]{8,20}-[a-z0-9]{3,16}$")
DOUBAO_SEED_2_PREFIXES = ("doubao-seed-2-", "doubao-seed-2.")
AUDIT_CATEGORY = "doubao_endpoints"


class DoubaoEndpointError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 409):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


def _utc(value: datetime | None) -> datetime | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    return value if value.tzinfo else value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def normalize_endpoint_id(value: Any) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_id_invalid", "接入点 ID 必须是字符串", status_code=422)
    candidate = value.strip()
    if not ENDPOINT_ID_PATTERN.match(candidate):
        raise DoubaoEndpointError(
            "doubao_endpoint_id_invalid",
            "接入点 ID 格式不合法，应形如 ep-20260507230659-skvgn",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return candidate


def is_doubao_seed_2_model(provider: Any, model_id: Any) -> bool:
    return (
        is_volcengine_ark_provider(provider)
        and isinstance(model_id, str)
        and model_id.strip().lower().startswith(DOUBAO_SEED_2_PREFIXES)
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 单例设置：接入点路由开关（账单相关字段由 volcengine_billing 维护）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def billing_settings(db: Session, *, create: bool = True) -> VolcengineBillingSettings | None:
    row = db.get(VolcengineBillingSettings, 1)
    if row is None and create:
        row = VolcengineBillingSettings(id=1)
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
    return row


def endpoint_routing_enabled(db: Session) -> bool:
    row = db.get(VolcengineBillingSettings, 1)
    return bool(row is not None and row.endpoint_routing_enabled)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 登记与绑定
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _binding_payload(definition: WorkflowDefinition) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "workflow_definition_id": definition.id,
        "workflow_key": definition.workflow_key,
        "workflow_name": definition.name,
        "workflow_status": definition.status,
        "inference_mode": definition.doubao_inference_mode,
    }


def bound_definitions(db: Session, endpoint_row_ids: list[int]) -> dict[int, list[WorkflowDefinition]]:
    """按接入点行 ID 汇总当前绑定的工作流（按工作流 ID 稳定排序）。"""

    grouped: dict[int, list[WorkflowDefinition]] = {row_id: [] for row_id in endpoint_row_ids}
    if not endpoint_row_ids:
        return grouped
    for definition in db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowDefinition)
        .where(or_(WorkflowDefinition.doubao_endpoint_row_id.in_(endpoint_row_ids), WorkflowDefinition.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id.in_(endpoint_row_ids)))
        .order_by(WorkflowDefinition.id)
    ).all():
        for row_id in (definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id, definition.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id):
            if row_id in grouped:
                grouped[row_id].append(definition)
    return grouped


def endpoint_payload(
    row: DoubaoInferenceEndpoint,
    *,
    definitions: list[WorkflowDefinition] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        verification = json.loads(row.verification_json or "{}")
    except ValueError:
        verification = {}
    try:
        tags = json.loads(getattr(row, "tags_json", "[]") or "[]")
    except ValueError:
        tags = []
    bindings = [_binding_payload(definition) for definition in (definitions or [])]
    return {
        "remote_name": getattr(row, "remote_name", "") or "",
        "provisioned_by_platform": bool(getattr(row, "provisioned_by_platform", False)),
        "tags": tags if isinstance(tags, list) else [],
        "id": row.id,
        "endpoint_id": row.endpoint_id,
        "inference_mode": "batch" if row.endpoint_id.startswith("ep-bi-") else "online",
        "display_name": row.display_name,
        "category_key": row.category_key,
        "bindings": bindings,
        "workflow_definition_ids": [item["workflow_definition_id"] for item in bindings],
        "foundation_model": row.foundation_model,
        "model_version": row.model_version,
        "status": row.status,
        "verification_status": row.verification_status,
        "verification": verification if isinstance(verification, dict) else {},
        "verified_at": _utc(row.verified_at),
        "notes": row.notes,
        "revision": row.revision,
        "created_by": row.created_by,
        "updated_by": row.updated_by,
        "created_at": _utc(row.created_at),
        "updated_at": _utc(row.updated_at),
    }


def endpoint_detail(db: Session, row: DoubaoInferenceEndpoint) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return endpoint_payload(row, definitions=bound_definitions(db, [row.id]).get(row.id, []))


def list_endpoints(db: Session, *, category_key: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    query = select(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint).order_by(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint.category_key, DoubaoInferenceEndpoint.id)
    if category_key:
        query = query.where(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint.category_key == category_key)
    rows = db.scalars(query).all()
    grouped = bound_definitions(db, [row.id for row in rows])
    return [endpoint_payload(row, definitions=grouped.get(row.id, [])) for row in rows]


def _require_category(db: Session, category_key: str) -> str:
    key = (category_key or "").strip()
    if not key:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_category_invalid", "类目不能为空", status_code=422)
    exists = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile.id).where(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == key)
    )
    if exists is None:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_category_unknown", f"类目 {key} 不存在", status_code=404)
    return key


def _audit(db: Session, row: DoubaoInferenceEndpoint, *, action: str, actor: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    append_audit_event(
        db,
        category=AUDIT_CATEGORY,
        action=action,
        subject_type="doubao_endpoint",
        subject_id=row.id,
        actor=actor,
        payload={"endpoint_id": row.endpoint_id, "revision": row.revision, **payload},
        event_key=f"doubao-endpoint:{row.id}:{action}:{row.revision}",
    )


def create_endpoint(
    db: Session,
    *,
    endpoint_id: str,
    display_name: str,
    category_key: str,
    foundation_model: str = "",
    model_version: str = "",
    notes: str = "",
    actor: str,
) -> DoubaoInferenceEndpoint:
    normalized = normalize_endpoint_id(endpoint_id)
    category = _require_category(db, category_key)
    if normalized.startswith('ep-bi-'):
        from .batch_inference import batch_pricing_contract
        try:
            batch_pricing_contract('doubao', foundation_model)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise DoubaoEndpointError('batch_model_unsupported', str(exc), status_code=422) from exc
    name = (display_name or "").strip()
    if not name:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_name_invalid", "接入点名称不能为空", status_code=422)
    duplicate = db.scalar(select(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint.id).where(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint.endpoint_id == normalized))
    if duplicate is not None:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_duplicate", f"接入点 {normalized} 已登记")
    row = DoubaoInferenceEndpoint(
        endpoint_id=normalized,
        display_name=name[:160],
        category_key=category,
        foundation_model=(foundation_model or "").strip()[:200],
        model_version=(model_version or "").strip()[:80],
        notes=(notes or "").strip(),
        created_by=actor,
        updated_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    _audit(db, row, action="created", actor=actor, payload={"category_key": category, "display_name": row.display_name})
    return row


_NAME_SAFE = re.compile(r"[^a-z0-9._-]+")


def provisioned_endpoint_name(*, deploy_environment: str, category_key: str, workflow_key: str) -> str:
    """火山侧名称：label-<env>-<类目>-<工作流 key>，便于在控制台按名称/标签识别。"""

    parts = [
        "label",
        _NAME_SAFE.sub("-", (deploy_environment or "local").lower()).strip("-") or "local",
        _NAME_SAFE.sub("-", category_key.lower()).strip("-"),
        _NAME_SAFE.sub("-", workflow_key.lower()).strip("-"),
    ]
    return "-".join(part for part in parts if part)[:128]


def provisioned_endpoint_tags(*, deploy_environment: str, category_key: str, workflow_key: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {
        "platform": "3d66-label-system",
        "managed_by": "label-system",
        "env": (deploy_environment or "local").lower(),
        "category": category_key,
        "workflow": workflow_key,
    }


def provision_endpoint(
    db: Session,
    *,
    client: Any,
    workflow_definition_id: int,
    category_key: str,
    foundation_model: str,
    model_version: str,
    deploy_environment: str,
    actor: str,
    display_name: str | None = None,
    rpm: int | None = None,
    tpm: int | None = None,
    project_name: str = "default",
    reason: str = "",
) -> DoubaoInferenceEndpoint:
    """经火山 CreateEndpoint 自动创建带标签的接入点，并立即绑定到类目工作流。

    先 ``DryRun`` 预检参数，再真正创建；创建成功后本地登记、绑定并写审计。
    火山侧创建是异步的：登记后状态为 ``unverified``，需等 Running 后点验收。
    """

    category = _require_category(db, category_key)
    definition = _lock_definition(db, workflow_definition_id)
    try:
        allowed = json.loads(definition.allowed_categories_json or "[]")
    except ValueError:
        allowed = []
    if category not in allowed:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError(
            "doubao_endpoint_category_mismatch",
            f"工作流 {definition.name} 不属于类目 {category}",
        )
    occupied = active_endpoint_for_definition(db, definition.id)
    if occupied is not None:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError(
            "doubao_endpoint_workflow_occupied",
            f"工作流 {definition.name} 已绑定接入点 {occupied.endpoint_id}，请先解绑或停用",
        )
    model = (foundation_model or "").strip()
    if not model:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_model_required", "底层模型不能为空", status_code=422)
    name = provisioned_endpoint_name(
        deploy_environment=deploy_environment, category_key=category, workflow_key=definition.workflow_key,
    )
    tags = provisioned_endpoint_tags(
        deploy_environment=deploy_environment, category_key=category, workflow_key=definition.workflow_key,
    )
    description = f"3d66 标签平台 {deploy_environment} · {category} · {definition.name}"[:500]
    common = dict(
        name=name,
        foundation_model=model,
        model_version=(model_version or "").strip(),
        description=description,
        tags=tags,
        rpm=rpm,
        tpm=tpm,
        project_name=project_name or "default",
    )
    client.create_endpoint(dry_run=True, **common)
    endpoint_id = client.create_endpoint(dry_run=False, **common)
    normalized = normalize_endpoint_id(endpoint_id)
    row = DoubaoInferenceEndpoint(
        endpoint_id=normalized,
        display_name=(display_name or "").strip()[:160] or f"{definition.name} · {deploy_environment}"[:160],
        category_key=category,
        foundation_model=model[:200],
        model_version=(model_version or "").strip()[:80],
        notes=(reason or "").strip(),
        remote_name=name,
        provisioned_by_platform=True,
        tags_json=json.dumps([{"Key": key, "Value": value} for key, value in tags.items()], ensure_ascii=False),
        created_by=actor,
        updated_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id = row.id
    db.flush()
    _audit(
        db,
        row,
        action="provisioned",
        actor=actor,
        payload={
            "workflow_definition_id": definition.id,
            "remote_name": name,
            "tags": tags,
            "foundation_model": model,
            "model_version": row.model_version,
            "rpm": rpm,
            "tpm": tpm,
            "reason": (reason or "").strip(),
        },
    )
    return row


def _locked_endpoint(db: Session, endpoint_row_id: int, *, expected_revision: int | None) -> DoubaoInferenceEndpoint:
    row = db.get(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint, endpoint_row_id)
    if row is None:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_missing", "接入点不存在", status_code=404)
    if expected_revision is not None and row.revision != expected_revision:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_revision_conflict", "接入点已被其他人修改，请刷新后重试")
    return row


def update_endpoint(
    db: Session,
    endpoint_row_id: int,
    *,
    expected_revision: int,
    actor: str,
    display_name: str | None = None,
    foundation_model: str | None = None,
    model_version: str | None = None,
    notes: str | None = None,
    status: str | None = None,
) -> DoubaoInferenceEndpoint:
    row = _locked_endpoint(db, endpoint_row_id, expected_revision=expected_revision)
    changes: dict[str, Any] = {}
    if display_name is not None:
        name = display_name.strip()
        if not name:
            raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_name_invalid", "接入点名称不能为空", status_code=422)
        changes["display_name"] = name[:160]
    if foundation_model is not None:
        changes["foundation_model"] = foundation_model.strip()[:200]
    if model_version is not None:
        changes["model_version"] = model_version.strip()[:80]
    if notes is not None:
        changes["notes"] = notes.strip()
    if status is not None:
        if status not in {"active", "disabled"}:
            raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_status_invalid", "状态只能是 active 或 disabled", status_code=422)
        changes["status"] = status
    for key, value in changes.items():
        setattr(row, key, value)
    row.revision += 1
    row.updated_by = actor
    db.flush()
    _audit(db, row, action="updated", actor=actor, payload={"changes": changes})
    return row


def _lock_definition(db: Session, definition_id: int) -> WorkflowDefinition:
    definition = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.id == definition_id).with_for_update()
    )
    if definition is None:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_workflow_missing", "工作流不存在", status_code=404)
    return definition


def _category_allows(definition: WorkflowDefinition, category_key: str) -> bool:
    try:
        allowed = json.loads(definition.allowed_categories_json or "[]")
    except ValueError:
        allowed = []
    return category_key in allowed


def bind_endpoint(
    db: Session,
    endpoint_row_id: int,
    *,
    workflow_definition_id: int,
    expected_revision: int,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
) -> DoubaoInferenceEndpoint:
    """把一个同类目工作流绑定到接入点；工作流已绑定其他接入点时直接改绑并留痕。"""

    if not (reason or "").strip():
        raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_reason_required", "绑定必须填写原因", status_code=422)
    row = _locked_endpoint(db, endpoint_row_id, expected_revision=expected_revision)
    if row.status != "active":
        raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_disabled", "已停用的接入点不能绑定工作流")
    definition = _lock_definition(db, workflow_definition_id)
    if not _category_allows(definition, row.category_key):
        raise DoubaoEndpointError(
            "doubao_endpoint_category_mismatch",
            f"工作流 {definition.name} 不属于类目 {row.category_key}",
        )
    slot = "doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id" if row.endpoint_id.startswith("ep-bi-") else "doubao_endpoint_row_id"
    previous = getattr(definition, slot)
    if previous == row.id:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError(
            "doubao_endpoint_already_bound",
            f"工作流 {definition.name} 已绑定接入点 {row.endpoint_id}",
        )
    previous_endpoint = db.get(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint, previous) if previous is not None else None
    setattr(definition, slot, row.id)
    row.revision += 1
    row.updated_by = actor
    db.flush()
    _audit(
        db,
        row,
        action="bound",
        actor=actor,
        payload={
            "workflow_definition_id": definition.id,
            "workflow_key": definition.workflow_key,
            "previous_endpoint_row_id": previous,
            "previous_endpoint_id": previous_endpoint.endpoint_id if previous_endpoint else None,
            "reason": reason.strip(),
        },
    )
    return row


def unbind_endpoint(
    db: Session,
    endpoint_row_id: int,
    *,
    workflow_definition_id: int,
    expected_revision: int,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
) -> DoubaoInferenceEndpoint:
    if not (reason or "").strip():
        raise DoubaoEndpointError("doubao_endpoint_reason_required", "解绑必须填写原因", status_code=422)
    row = _locked_endpoint(db, endpoint_row_id, expected_revision=expected_revision)
    definition = _lock_definition(db, workflow_definition_id)
    slot = "doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id" if row.endpoint_id.startswith("ep-bi-") else "doubao_endpoint_row_id"
    if getattr(definition, slot) != row.id:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError(
            "doubao_endpoint_not_bound",
            f"工作流 {definition.name} 未绑定接入点 {row.endpoint_id}",
        )
    setattr(definition, slot, None)
    row.revision += 1
    row.updated_by = actor
    db.flush()
    _audit(
        db,
        row,
        action="unbound",
        actor=actor,
        payload={"workflow_definition_id": definition.id, "workflow_key": definition.workflow_key, "reason": reason.strip()},
    )
    return row


def record_verification(
    db: Session,
    endpoint_row_id: int,
    *,
    actor: str,
    ok: bool,
    detail: Mapping[str, Any],
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> DoubaoInferenceEndpoint:
    row = _locked_endpoint(db, endpoint_row_id, expected_revision=None)
    row.verification_status = "verified" if ok else "failed"
    row.verification_json = json.dumps(dict(detail), ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True)
    row.verified_at = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    row.revision += 1
    row.updated_by = actor
    db.flush()
    _audit(db, row, action="verified" if ok else "verification_failed", actor=actor, payload={"detail": dict(detail)})
    return row


def evaluate_remote_endpoint(row: DoubaoInferenceEndpoint, remote: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[bool, dict[str, Any]]:
    """把 GetEndpoint 的结果压成可展示的验收结论。"""

    reference = remote.get("ModelReference") if isinstance(remote.get("ModelReference"), Mapping) else {}
    foundation = reference.get("FoundationModel") if isinstance(reference.get("FoundationModel"), Mapping) else {}
    remote_model = str(foundation.get("Name") or reference.get("CustomModelId") or "")
    remote_version = str(foundation.get("ModelVersion") or "")
    status = str(remote.get("Status") or "")
    rate_limit = remote.get("RateLimit") if isinstance(remote.get("RateLimit"), Mapping) else {}
    problems: list[str] = []
    if status != "Running":
        problems.append(f"接入点状态为 {status or '未知'}，不是 Running")
    if row.foundation_model and remote_model and row.foundation_model.strip().lower() != remote_model.strip().lower():
        problems.append(f"登记模型 {row.foundation_model} 与火山侧 {remote_model} 不一致")
    if row.model_version and row.model_version.strip() != remote_version.strip():
        problems.append(f"登记模型版本 {row.model_version} 与火山侧 {remote_version or '未知'} 不一致")
    detail = {
        "checked": "GetEndpoint",
        "status": status,
        "status_reason": str(remote.get("StatusReason") or ""),
        "name": str(remote.get("Name") or ""),
        "foundation_model": remote_model,
        "model_version": remote_version,
        "project_name": str(remote.get("ProjectName") or ""),
        "rate_limit_rpm": rate_limit.get("Rpm"),
        "rate_limit_tpm": rate_limit.get("Tpm"),
        "problems": problems,
    }
    return not problems, detail


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 工作流就绪判定与运行时解析
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def manifest_model_registry_ids(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[int]:
    ids: list[int] = []
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", manifest.get("steps", [])) or []:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        config = node.get("config") if isinstance(node.get("config"), Mapping) else {}
        candidates = [config]
        item_executor = config.get("item_executor") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
        if isinstance(item_executor, Mapping):
            candidates.append(item_executor)
        for candidate in candidates:
            value = candidate.get("model_registry_id")
            if isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool) and value not in ids:
                ids.append(value)
    return ids


def manifest_requires_doubao_endpoint(db: Session, manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    ids = manifest_model_registry_ids(manifest)
    if not ids:
        return False
    for entry in db.scalars(select(ModelRegistryEntry).where(ModelRegistryEntry.id.in_(ids))).all():
        if is_doubao_seed_2_model(entry.provider, entry.model_id):
            return True
    return False


def active_endpoint_for_definition(db: Session, workflow_definition_id: int | None) -> DoubaoInferenceEndpoint | None:
    if workflow_definition_id is None:
        return None
    definition = db.get(WorkflowDefinition, workflow_definition_id)
    if definition is None or definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id is None:
        return None
    row = db.get(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint, definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id)
    if row is None or row.status != "active":
        return None
    return row


def _endpoint_summary(row: DoubaoInferenceEndpoint | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if row is None:
        return None
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "endpoint_id": row.endpoint_id,
        "inference_mode": "batch" if row.endpoint_id.startswith("ep-bi-") else "online",
        "display_name": row.display_name,
        "status": row.status,
        "verification_status": row.verification_status,
        "foundation_model": row.foundation_model,
    }


def endpoint_binding_summary(db: Session, workflow_definition_id: int | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    return _endpoint_summary(active_endpoint_for_definition(db, workflow_definition_id))


def endpoint_route_summary(db: Session, workflow_definition_id: int | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """这一轮真正会打的接入点，外加在线兜底位。

    给只读展示用。``endpoint_binding_summary`` 读的是 ``doubao_endpoint_row_id``——
    那是在线位，钉死批量的工作流它照样返回在线行。2026-09-16 实测：3D/SU 当天
    134 条运行 100% 冻结成 batch，增量自动化卡片却把在线接入点显示成日常通道。
    这里按 ``select_inference_route`` 的解析结果选行，让展示和冻结走同一套判定。

    ``require_endpoint=False``：卡片一次调用都不会发起，没绑批量接入点该照实显示
    「未绑定」而不是整块拿不到信息；真正发起付费调用的路径仍是默认的严格解析。
    """

    if workflow_definition_id is None:
        return None
    try:
        route = select_inference_route(db, workflow_definition_id, offline=True,
                                       require_endpoint=False)
    except DoubaoEndpointError as exc:
        # 接入点停用、兜底不可用这类是真实的配置问题。照实说出来，不要退回去
        # 显示一条看着正常的在线行把问题盖住。
        return {"mode": None, "primary": None, "fallback": None, "error": str(exc)}
    online = (route.get("fallback_policy") or {}).get("online_route") or {}
    return {
        "mode": route["mode"],
        "primary": _endpoint_summary(_endpoint_by_id(db, route.get("endpoint_id"))),
        "fallback": _endpoint_summary(_endpoint_by_id(db, online.get("endpoint_id"))),
        "error": None,
    }


def _endpoint_by_id(db: Session, endpoint_id: str | None) -> DoubaoInferenceEndpoint | None:
    """按 ep- 号取行。用解析结果反查，避免在这里重抄一遍选行规则。"""

    if not endpoint_id:
        return None
    return db.scalar(select(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint)
                     .where(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint.endpoint_id == endpoint_id))


def endpoint_readiness_blocker(db: Session, manifest: Mapping[str, Any], workflow_definition_id: int | None, *, inference_mode: str = "auto") -> str | None:
    """路由开关打开且工作流使用豆包 2.0 时，缺少接入点即阻塞。"""

    if not endpoint_routing_enabled(db):
        return None
    if not manifest_requires_doubao_endpoint(db, manifest):
        return None
    try:
        route = select_inference_route(db, workflow_definition_id, mode=inference_mode, offline=True)
    except DoubaoEndpointError as exc:
        return str(exc)
    if not route.get("endpoint_id"):
        return "接入点路由已启用，但该工作流尚未绑定生效中的豆包推理接入点，请在工作流管理中绑定"
    return None


def resolve_run_endpoint(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    *,
    provider: Any,
    model_id: Any,
    inference_route: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> str | None:
    """返回本次运行应使用的接入点 ID，并在首次调用时冻结到运行上。

    - 运行已冻结接入点：始终沿用，即使之后改绑或停用；
    - 模型不是豆包 2.0（如 1.8 旧链路）：忽略绑定，直连模型 ID；
    - 未冻结且当前有生效绑定：冻结并返回；
    - 路由开关打开且模型为豆包 2.0 却无接入点：抛错，禁止发起付费调用；
    - 其余情况返回 None，沿用 model_id 直连（旧路径）。
    """

    if not is_doubao_seed_2_model(provider, model_id):
        return None
    if inference_route is not None:
        endpoint = inference_route.get('endpoint_id')
        if inference_route.get('mode') == 'batch':
            from .batch_inference import batch_pricing_contract
            try:
                batch_pricing_contract(provider, model_id)
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise DoubaoEndpointError('batch_model_unsupported', str(exc)) from exc
            if not endpoint or not endpoint.startswith('ep-bi-'):
                raise DoubaoEndpointError('batch_route_invalid', '冻结批量接入点不合法')
            if inference_route.get('foundation_model') != model_id:
                raise DoubaoEndpointError('batch_model_mismatch', '批量接入点模型与工作流冻结模型不一致')
        if not endpoint and endpoint_routing_enabled(db):
            raise DoubaoEndpointError('doubao_endpoint_missing', '接入点路由已启用，冻结运行缺少接入点，已拒绝付费调用')
        if endpoint:
            normalize_endpoint_id(endpoint)
            run.doubao_endpoint_id = endpoint
        return endpoint
    frozen = getattr(run, "doubao_endpoint_id", None)
    if isinstance(frozen, str) and frozen:
        return frozen
    if not is_volcengine_ark_provider(provider):
        return None
    if not is_doubao_seed_2_model(provider, model_id):
        # 接入点承载的是豆包 2.0；同类目里的 1.8 旧链路工作流即使绑定了接入点也保持模型 ID 直连，
        # 否则请求会被路由到接入点背后的 2.0 模型，悄悄换掉对照模型。
        return None
    bound = active_endpoint_for_definition(db, run.workflow_definition_id)
    if bound is not None:
        run.doubao_endpoint_id = bound.endpoint_id
        return bound.endpoint_id
    if endpoint_routing_enabled(db):
        raise DoubaoEndpointError(
            "doubao_endpoint_missing",
            "接入点路由已启用，该类目工作流未绑定豆包推理接入点，已拒绝发起付费调用",
        )
    return None


def _canonical_foundation_model(row: DoubaoInferenceEndpoint) -> str:
    """Resolve explicitly registered version metadata, never infer a release."""
    name = (row.foundation_model or '').strip()
    version = (row.model_version or '').strip()
    if not version or name.endswith('-' + version):
        return name
    # A full model ID plus conflicting metadata must not become an alias.
    if re.search(r'-[0-9]{6}$', name):
        return ''
    return f'{name}-{version}' if name else ''


def workflow_route_summaries(db: Session, *, category_key: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Read-only display of the same readiness gate used when freezing new runs."""
    from .batch_endpoint_recovery import online_escalation_enabled
    query = select(WorkflowDefinition).order_by(WorkflowDefinition.id)
    enabled = os.environ.get('LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_ONLINE_FALLBACK', '').lower() == 'true'
    # 兜底编排（冻结 fallback_policy）与升级动作（满 24 小时真的转在线）是两个开关：
    # 这里两个都报出来，否则运营在页面上看到「双接入点已就绪」，却不知道升级已被停用。
    escalation = online_escalation_enabled()
    rows = []
    for definition in db.scalars(query):
        if category_key and not _category_allows(definition, category_key):
            continue
        row = {
            'workflow_definition_id': definition.id,
            'inference_mode': definition.doubao_inference_mode,
            'batch_endpoint_row_id': definition.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id,
            'online_endpoint_row_id': definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id,
            'fallback_policy_enabled': enabled,
            'online_escalation_enabled': escalation,
            'fallback_ready': False,
            'blocker': None,
        }
        if not enabled:
            row['blocker'] = '服务尚未启用在线兜底策略'
        else:
            try:
                route = select_inference_route(db, definition.id, mode='batch', offline=True)
                batch = db.get(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint, definition.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id)
                if batch.verification_status != 'verified':
                    row['blocker'] = '批量接入点尚未验收'
                else:
                    policy = route['fallback_policy']
                    row.update(fallback_ready=True, batch_max_attempts=policy['batch_max_attempts'], deadline_seconds=policy['deadline_seconds'])
            except DoubaoEndpointError as exc:
                row['blocker'] = str(exc)
        rows.append(row)
    return rows


def select_inference_route(db, workflow_definition_id, *, mode='auto', offline=False,
                           require_endpoint=True, allow_online_fallback=True):
    """解析一条 ``inference-route-v1``。

    ``require_endpoint=False`` 只给一次供应商调用都不会发起的路径使用（例如存量回归
    的快速模拟）：它们同样要按开关冻结 ``mode``，但不该因为工作流没绑批量接入点就被
    挡住。真正发起付费调用的路径一律保持默认的严格解析。

    ``allow_online_fallback=False`` 给「调用方自己点名了批量」的路径使用。全局兜底
    开关只知道解析结果是 batch，不知道这是平台默认策略挑出来的批量、还是运营明确选
    的批量；这两者的期望完全不同。运营选了批量就是这一轮全程批量，不能在 3 次尝试
    用尽或 24 小时到期后被换成在线（在线价是批量的 1.845 倍）。默认 ``True``，共享
    的 auto 策略与其他调用方行为不变。
    """
    if mode not in {'auto', 'online', 'batch'}:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError('inference_mode_invalid', '推理方式必须为auto、online或batch', status_code=422)
    definition = db.get(WorkflowDefinition, workflow_definition_id)
    if definition is None:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError('doubao_endpoint_workflow_missing', '工作流不存在', status_code=404)
    selected = definition.doubao_inference_mode if mode == 'auto' and offline else mode
    # 不要因为「定义钉死了 batch」就摘掉 fallback_policy：它不只是「失败转在线」，
    # 而是启用整套批量传输编排（提交等待、节流、24 小时截止）的那把钥匙——
    # workflow_fixture_executor 以 recovery.policy(runtime) 是否为 None 决定走不走
    # 状态机。2026-09-15 实测摘掉之后批量退化成一次普通 HTTP 直连，2/2 全部挂到
    # 600 秒客户端超时并留下 model_invocation_outcome_unknown。
    # 「只走批量不转在线」另有 batch_experiment.batch_only_experiment（按 run 的运营
    # 操作）；兜底带来的报价上浮只抬高冻结上限，由预留侧按预期用量解决，不在这里摘。
    if selected == 'auto':
        selected = 'batch' if offline and definition.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id else 'online'
    row_id = definition.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id if selected == 'batch' else definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id
    row = db.get(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint, row_id) if row_id else None
    if row is not None and row.status != 'active':
        raise DoubaoEndpointError('doubao_endpoint_disabled', '选中的推理接入点已停用')
    if selected == 'batch' and row is None and require_endpoint:
        raise DoubaoEndpointError('doubao_batch_endpoint_missing', '该工作流尚未绑定批量接入点')
    route = {'schema_version':'inference-route-v1','mode':selected,'endpoint_id':row.endpoint_id if row else None,
            'foundation_model':row.foundation_model if row else None, 'endpoint_revision':row.revision if row else None}

    if (selected == 'batch' and row is not None and allow_online_fallback
            and os.environ.get('LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_ONLINE_FALLBACK', '').lower() == 'true'):
        online = db.get(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint, definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id) if definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id else None
        if (online is None or online.status != 'active'
                or online.verification_status != 'verified'
                or not online.endpoint_id.startswith('ep-')
                or online.endpoint_id.startswith('ep-bi-')
                or not online.foundation_model
                or _canonical_foundation_model(online) != _canonical_foundation_model(row)):
            raise DoubaoEndpointError('batch_online_fallback_unavailable', '批量在线兜底需要同工作流绑定已验收、启用且底层模型相同的在线接入点')
        normalize_endpoint_id(online.endpoint_id)
        route['fallback_policy'] = {
            'version': 'batch-online-fallback-v1',
            'batch_max_attempts': 3,
            'deadline_seconds': 86400,
            'online_route': {
                'schema_version': 'inference-route-v1', 'mode': 'online',
                'endpoint_id': online.endpoint_id, 'foundation_model': _canonical_foundation_model(online),
                'endpoint_revision': online.revision,
            },
        }
    return route


def assert_explicit_route_ready(db, manifest, route, *, inference_mode):
    """显式选定在线或批量时，在发起付费调用之前拒绝配置不全的路由。

    ``auto`` 是既有的兼容入口，保持原行为不变。显式选定时，缺接入点原先要等到
    ``resolve_run_endpoint`` 真正发请求才暴露；那时 run 已经建好、素材已经排队，
    运营看到的是一整批失败而不是一次「不能启动」。这里把同一个判定提前到建 run 与
    费用预估，判定条件与真实调用侧完全一致：接入点路由已启用、且该机制确实使用
    豆包 2.0 时才要求接入点。
    """

    if inference_mode not in {'online', 'batch'}:
        return
    if route.get('endpoint_id'):
        return
    if not endpoint_routing_enabled(db):
        return
    if not manifest_requires_doubao_endpoint(db, manifest):
        return
    if route.get('mode') == 'online':
        raise DoubaoEndpointError(
            'doubao_online_endpoint_missing',
            '本轮选择了在线推理，但该工作流没有生效中的在线接入点，请先在工作流管理中绑定后再启动',
            status_code=422,
        )
    raise DoubaoEndpointError(
        'doubao_batch_endpoint_missing', '该工作流尚未绑定批量接入点', status_code=422
    )

===== FILE backend/app/doubao_endpoints_api.py =====
"""豆包推理接入点登记/绑定与火山账单对账的运营 API。

- 读取：具备 ``models:read`` 的成员可查看接入点与成本看板；
- 变更：接入点登记、绑定、账单设置与开关需要系统管理员。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Callable, Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Path, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, SecretStr
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError, OperationalError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .authz import has_permission
from .database import get_db
from .doubao_endpoint_models import DoubaoInferenceEndpoint
from .doubao_endpoints import (
    DoubaoEndpointError,
    bind_endpoint,
    billing_settings,
    create_endpoint,
    endpoint_detail,
    evaluate_remote_endpoint,
    list_endpoints,
    provision_endpoint,
    record_verification,
    unbind_endpoint,
    update_endpoint,
    workflow_route_summaries,
)
from .models import User
from .volcengine_ark_openapi import VolcengineOpenApiError
from .volcengine_billing import (
    VolcengineBillingError,
    add_adjustment,
    billing_dashboard,
    build_client,
    list_reconciliations,
    run_usage_sync,
    settings_payload,
    update_settings,
)


class InferenceModeRequest(BaseModel):
    mode: Literal['auto', 'online', 'batch']
    expected_mode: Literal['auto', 'online', 'batch']


class EndpointCreateRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    endpoint_id: str = Field(min_length=6, max_length=80)
    display_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    category_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    foundation_model: str = Field(default="", max_length=200)
    model_version: str = Field(default="", max_length=80)
    notes: str = Field(default="", max_length=4000)


class EndpointProvisionRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    workflow_definition_id: int = Field(ge=1, strict=True)
    category_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    foundation_model: str = Field(default="doubao-seed-2-0-lite", min_length=1, max_length=200)
    model_version: str = Field(default="", max_length=80)
    display_name: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=160)
    rpm: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1, le=1_000_000)
    tpm: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1, le=1_000_000_000)
    reason: str = Field(default="", max_length=2000)


class EndpointUpdateRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=1, strict=True)
    display_name: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=160)
    foundation_model: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=200)
    model_version: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=80)
    notes: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=4000)
    status: str | None = Field(default=None, pattern=r"^(active|disabled)$")


class EndpointBindRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    workflow_definition_id: int = Field(ge=1, strict=True)
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=1, strict=True)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


class EndpointUnbindRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    workflow_definition_id: int = Field(ge=1, strict=True)
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=1, strict=True)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


class BillingSettingsRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=1, strict=True)
    sync_enabled: bool | None = None
    endpoint_routing_enabled: bool | None = None
    access_key: SecretStr | None = None
    secret_key: SecretStr | None = None
    region: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=40)

    sync_interval_seconds: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=300, le=86_400)

    reason: str = Field(default="", max_length=2000)


class AdjustmentRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

def _http(exc: DoubaoEndpointError | VolcengineBillingError | VolcengineOpenApiError) -> HTTPException:
    status_code = getattr(exc, "status_code", None) or 409
    if isinstance(exc, VolcengineOpenApiError):
        status_code = 502
    return HTTPException(status_code=status_code, detail={"code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)})


def build_doubao_endpoints_router(require_user: Callable[..., Any]) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/doubao-endpoints", tags=["doubao-endpoints"])

    def _reader(user: User = Depends(require_user)) -> User:
        if not has_permission(user, "models:read"):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail={"code": "forbidden", "message": "缺少权限：models:read"})
        return user

    def _admin(user: User = Depends(require_user)) -> User:
        if not (user.is_admin or has_permission(user, "*")):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail={"code": "forbidden", "message": "仅系统管理员可维护豆包接入点与账单设置"})
        return user

    def _definition_payload(db: Session, row: DoubaoInferenceEndpoint) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return endpoint_detail(db, row)

    # ---- 接入点 ---------------------------------------------------------

    @router.get("")
    def get_endpoints(
        category_key: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=40),
        user: User = Depends(_reader),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        settings = billing_settings(db, create=False)
        return {
            "items": list_endpoints(db, category_key=category_key),
            "can_manage": bool(user.is_admin or has_permission(user, "*")),
            "endpoint_routing_enabled": bool(settings and settings.endpoint_routing_enabled),
            "workflow_routes": workflow_route_summaries(db, category_key=category_key),
        }

    @router.put('/workflows/{definition_id}/inference-mode')
    def put_inference_mode(definition_id: int, payload: InferenceModeRequest, user: User = Depends(_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        from .doubao_endpoints import _lock_definition, select_inference_route
        from .audit import append_audit_event
        try:
            row = _lock_definition(db, definition_id)
            if row.doubao_inference_mode != payload.expected_mode:
                raise DoubaoEndpointError('inference_mode_conflict', '工作流推理策略已变化，请刷新')
            select_inference_route(db, definition_id, mode=payload.mode, offline=True)
            row.doubao_inference_mode = payload.mode
            append_audit_event(db, category='doubao_endpoints', action='inference_mode_changed',
                subject_type='workflow_definition', subject_id=row.id, actor=user.username,
                payload={'previous':payload.expected_mode, 'mode':payload.mode})
            db.commit()
            return {'workflow_definition_id':row.id,'inference_mode':row.doubao_inference_mode}
        except DoubaoEndpointError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.post("", status_code=201)
    def post_endpoint(payload: EndpointCreateRequest, user: User = Depends(_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            row = create_endpoint(db, actor=user.username, **payload.model_dump())
            db.commit()
            return _definition_payload(db, row)
        except DoubaoEndpointError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        except IntegrityError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail={"code": "doubao_endpoint_duplicate", "message": "接入点已存在"}) from exc

    @router.post("/provision", status_code=201)
    def post_provision(payload: EndpointProvisionRequest, user: User = Depends(_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """经火山 CreateEndpoint 自动创建带标签的接入点并绑定到类目工作流。"""

        from .config import get_settings

        settings = billing_settings(db)
        try:
            client = build_client(settings)
            row = provision_endpoint(
                db,
                client=client,
                deploy_environment=get_settings().deploy_environment,
                actor=user.username,
                project_name=settings.project_name or "default",
                **payload.model_dump(),
            )
            db.commit()
            return _definition_payload(db, row)
        except (DoubaoEndpointError, VolcengineBillingError, VolcengineOpenApiError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        except IntegrityError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail={"code": "doubao_endpoint_duplicate", "message": "接入点已存在"}) from exc

    @router.put("/{endpoint_row_id}")
    def put_endpoint(payload: EndpointUpdateRequest, endpoint_row_id: int = Path(ge=1), user: User = Depends(_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            row = update_endpoint(db, endpoint_row_id, actor=user.username, **payload.model_dump())
            db.commit()
            return _definition_payload(db, row)
        except DoubaoEndpointError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{endpoint_row_id}/bind")
    def post_bind(payload: EndpointBindRequest, endpoint_row_id: int = Path(ge=1), user: User = Depends(_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            row = bind_endpoint(db, endpoint_row_id, actor=user.username, **payload.model_dump())
            db.commit()
            return _definition_payload(db, row)
        except DoubaoEndpointError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        except (IntegrityError, OperationalError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail={"code": "doubao_endpoint_conflict", "message": "并发操作冲突，请刷新后重试"}) from exc

    @router.post("/{endpoint_row_id}/unbind")
    def post_unbind(payload: EndpointUnbindRequest, endpoint_row_id: int = Path(ge=1), user: User = Depends(_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            row = unbind_endpoint(db, endpoint_row_id, actor=user.username, **payload.model_dump())
            db.commit()
            return _definition_payload(db, row)
        except DoubaoEndpointError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{endpoint_row_id}/verify")
    def post_verify(endpoint_row_id: int = Path(ge=1), user: User = Depends(_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """用只读 AK/SK 调 GetEndpoint 做录入校验；不发起任何付费推理。"""

        row = db.get(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint, endpoint_row_id)
        if row is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail={"code": "doubao_endpoint_missing", "message": "接入点不存在"})
        settings = billing_settings(db)
        try:
            client = build_client(settings)
            remote = client.get_endpoint(row.endpoint_id)
            ok, detail = evaluate_remote_endpoint(row, remote)
        except VolcengineBillingError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        except VolcengineOpenApiError as exc:
            ok, detail = False, {"checked": "GetEndpoint", "problems": [str(exc)], "error_code": exc.code}
        try:
            row = record_verification(db, endpoint_row_id, actor=user.username, ok=ok, detail=detail)
            db.commit()
        except DoubaoEndpointError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        return _definition_payload(db, row)

    # ---- 账单设置与对账 -------------------------------------------------

    @router.get("/billing/settings")
    def get_billing_settings(_user: User = Depends(_reader), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = billing_settings(db)
        db.commit()
        return settings_payload(row)

    @router.put("/billing/settings")
    def put_billing_settings(payload: BillingSettingsRequest, user: User = Depends(_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        data = payload.model_dump()
        data["access_key"] = payload.access_key.get_secret_value() if payload.access_key else None
        data["secret_key"] = payload.secret_key.get_secret_value() if payload.secret_key else None
        try:
            row = update_settings(db, actor=user.username, **data)
            db.commit()
            return settings_payload(row)
        except VolcengineBillingError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/billing/sync")
    def post_billing_sync(user: User = Depends(_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """人工触发一次拉取与对账；失败结果同样落库并返回。"""

        settings = billing_settings(db)
        if not (settings.encrypted_access_key and settings.encrypted_secret_key):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail={"code": "volcengine_credentials_missing", "message": "请先保存只读 AK/SK"})
        run = run_usage_sync(db, actor=user.username, trigger="manual", now=datetime.now(timezone.utc))
        db.commit()
        return {
            "id": run.id,
            "status": run.status,
            "rows_upserted": run.rows_upserted,
            "reconciliations_frozen": run.reconciliations_frozen,
            "error_code": run.error_code,
            "error_message": run.error_message,
            "window_start_date": run.window_start_date,
            "window_end_date": run.window_end_date,
        }

    @router.get("/billing/dashboard")
    def get_billing_dashboard(_user: User = Depends(_reader), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return billing_dashboard(db)

    @router.get("/billing/reconciliations")
    def get_reconciliations(
        endpoint_id: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=80),
        date_from: str | None = Query(default=None, pattern=r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$"),
        date_to: str | None = Query(default=None, pattern=r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$"),
        limit: int = Query(default=200, ge=1, le=1000),
        _user: User = Depends(_reader),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"items": list_reconciliations(db, endpoint_id=endpoint_id, date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to, limit=limit)}

    @router.post("/billing/reconciliations/{reconciliation_id}/adjustments")
    def post_adjustment(payload: AdjustmentRequest, reconciliation_id: int = Path(ge=1), user: User = Depends(_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        from .volcengine_billing import reconciliation_payload

        try:
            row = add_adjustment(db, reconciliation_id, actor=user.username, note=payload.note)
            db.commit()
            return reconciliation_payload(row)
        except VolcengineBillingError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/downstream_publish_ledger.py =====
"""下游写入变更日志（ADR-0064）。

下游目标表按素材原地覆盖，只留最新一行；平台侧本表按 revision 追加，每次发布一行，
记录触发方（自动运行 / 人工纠偏）、操作者、原因、上一版指纹、字段级变更和投递结果。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Mapping
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .api_datetime_encoding import platform_isoformat
from .audit import canonical_json
from .models import DownstreamPublishLedger, RuntimeOutboxEvent

TRIGGER_KINDS = frozenset({"auto_run", "human_correction"})
_MAX_CHANGED_PATHS = 200


def diff_result_paths(
    previous: Any,
    current: Any,
    *,
    prefix: str = "",
) -> list[str]:
    """列出两版结果之间发生变化的字段路径（点号路径，数组按整体比较）。"""

    if isinstance(previous, Mapping) and isinstance(current, Mapping):
        paths: list[str] = []
        for key in sorted(set(previous) | set(current), key=str):
            child = f"{prefix}.{key}" if prefix else str(key)
            if key not in previous or key not in current:
                paths.append(child)
            else:
                paths.extend(
                    diff_result_paths(previous[key], current[key], prefix=child)
                )
            if len(paths) >= _MAX_CHANGED_PATHS:
                return paths[:_MAX_CHANGED_PATHS]
        return paths
    if previous == current:
        return []
    return [prefix or "$"]


def build_change_record(
    *,
    trigger_kind: str,
    actor: str,
    reason: str = "",
    correction_event_id: str = "",
    previous: RuntimeOutboxEvent | None,
    previous_result: Any,
    current_result: Any,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """生成随 Outbox payload 冻结的变更说明（也是本表的来源）。"""

    if trigger_kind not in TRIGGER_KINDS:
        raise ValueError("trigger_kind 只能是 auto_run 或 human_correction")
    return {
        "schema_version": "downstream-change-record-v1",
        "trigger_kind": trigger_kind,
        "actor": (actor or "").strip()[:120],
        "reason": (reason or "").strip(),
        "correction_event_id": (correction_event_id or "")[:120],
        "previous_revision": int(previous.revision) if previous is not None else None,
        "previous_payload_hash": previous.payload_hash if previous is not None else "",
        "previous_review_state": previous.review_state if previous is not None else None,
        "changed_paths": (
            diff_result_paths(previous_result, current_result)
            if previous is not None
            else []
        ),
    }


def record_downstream_publish(
    db: Session,
    *,
    event: RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    payload: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> DownstreamPublishLedger:
    """按 Outbox 事件追加一行变更日志；同一事件重复调用只返回已有行。"""

    existing = db.scalar(
        select(DownstreamPublishLedger).where(
            DownstreamPublishLedger.outbox_event_id == event.id
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    change = payload.get("change")
    change = change if isinstance(change, Mapping) else {}
    trigger_kind = str(change.get("trigger_kind") or "auto_run")
    if trigger_kind not in TRIGGER_KINDS:
        trigger_kind = "auto_run"
    previous_revision = change.get("previous_revision")
    source_id = payload.get("source_id")
    row = DownstreamPublishLedger(
        outbox_event_id=event.id,
        target_contract_key=event.target_contract_key,
        target_contract_version_id=event.target_contract_version_id,
        business_idempotency_key=event.business_idempotency_key,
        revision=int(event.revision or 1),
        review_state=str(event.review_state or "auto"),
        category_key=(
            str(payload.get("category_key"))[:40]
            if payload.get("category_key") is not None
            else None
        ),
        source_type=(
            str(payload.get("source_type"))[:40]
            if payload.get("source_type") is not None
            else None
        ),
        source_id=(
            int(source_id)
            if isinstance(source_id, int) and not isinstance(source_id, bool)
            else None
        ),
        asset_identity=str(payload.get("asset_identity") or "")[:240],
        mechanism_version_id=int(event.mechanism_version_id),
        origin_run_key=str(payload.get("origin_run_key") or "")[:160],
        node_instance_id=str(event.node_instance_id or "")[:160],
        trigger_kind=trigger_kind,
        actor=str(change.get("actor") or "")[:120],
        reason=str(change.get("reason") or ""),
        correction_event_id=str(change.get("correction_event_id") or "")[:120],
        previous_revision=(
            int(previous_revision)
            if isinstance(previous_revision, int) and not isinstance(previous_revision, bool)
            else None
        ),
        previous_payload_hash=str(change.get("previous_payload_hash") or "")[:64],
        changed_paths_json=canonical_json(list(change.get("changed_paths") or [])),
        payload_json=canonical_json(dict(payload)),
        payload_hash=event.payload_hash,
        delivery_status="pending",
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    # 素材大宽表（ADR-0111）：按 origin_run_key 找回运行与素材；失败只记日志。
    from .wide_material import refresh_for_run_key

    refresh_for_run_key(db, row.origin_run_key, event_kind="delivered")
    return row


def mark_downstream_publish_outcome(
    db: Session,
    *,
    outbox_event_id: int,
    delivery_status: str,
    external_record_key: str = "",
    rejection_code: str = "",
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> DownstreamPublishLedger | None:
    """投递器回写结果：delivered / retrying / rejected（护栏或身份冲突）/ dead_lettered。"""

    row = db.scalar(
        select(DownstreamPublishLedger).where(
            DownstreamPublishLedger.outbox_event_id == outbox_event_id
        )
    )
    if row is None:
        return None
    row.delivery_status = delivery_status
    row.rejection_code = (rejection_code or "")[:80]
    if external_record_key:
        row.external_record_key = external_record_key[:240]
    if delivery_status == "delivered":
        row.delivered_at = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    db.flush()
    from .wide_material import refresh_for_run_key

    refresh_for_run_key(db, row.origin_run_key, event_kind="delivered")
    return row


def ledger_row_as_dict(row: DownstreamPublishLedger, *, include_payload: bool) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        changed_paths = json.loads(row.changed_paths_json or "[]")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        changed_paths = []
    item: dict[str, Any] = {
        "id": row.id,
        "outbox_event_id": row.outbox_event_id,
        "target_contract_key": row.target_contract_key,
        "target_contract_version_id": row.target_contract_version_id,
        "business_idempotency_key": row.business_idempotency_key,
        "revision": row.revision,
        "review_state": row.review_state,
        "category_key": row.category_key,
        "source_type": row.source_type,
        "source_id": row.source_id,
        "asset_identity": row.asset_identity,
        "mechanism_version_id": row.mechanism_version_id,
        "origin_run_key": row.origin_run_key,
        "node_instance_id": row.node_instance_id,
        "trigger_kind": row.trigger_kind,
        "actor": row.actor,
        "reason": row.reason,
        "correction_event_id": row.correction_event_id,
        "previous_revision": row.previous_revision,
        "previous_payload_hash": row.previous_payload_hash,
        "changed_paths": changed_paths,
        "payload_hash": row.payload_hash,
        "delivery_status": row.delivery_status,
        "rejection_code": row.rejection_code,
        "external_record_key": row.external_record_key,
        "delivered_at": platform_isoformat(row.delivered_at) if row.delivered_at else None,
        "created_at": platform_isoformat(row.created_at) if row.created_at else None,
    }
    if include_payload:
        try:
            item["payload"] = json.loads(row.payload_json or "{}")
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            item["payload"] = {}
    return item

===== FILE backend/app/effective_batch_clock.py =====
"""Audited maintenance-adjusted fallback clock, separate from frozen facts.

Only explicit maintenance records stop time. Queueing, provider backoff and
ordinary worker restarts do not. No provider result or financial row is edited.
"""
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import json
from sqlalchemy import and_, func, or_, select
from .audit import append_audit_event
from .batch_endpoint_recovery import utc
from .models import AuditEvent, ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowModelInvocation

ACTIONS = ('maintenance_pause', 'maintenance_resume', 'batch_window_restart',
           'release_admission_pause', 'release_admission_resume')


def effective_queue_time(db, *, run_id, queued_at, now):
    start, end = utc(queued_at), utc(now)
    rows = db.scalars(select(AuditEvent).where(
        or_(and_(AuditEvent.subject_type == 'production_run', AuditEvent.subject_id == str(run_id)),
            and_(AuditEvent.subject_type == 'runtime', AuditEvent.subject_id == 'all')),
        AuditEvent.action.in_(ACTIONS), AuditEvent.created_at <= end,
    ).order_by(AuditEvent.created_at, AuditEvent.id)).all()
    for row in rows:
        if row.action == 'batch_window_restart' and row.subject_type == 'production_run':
            start = max(start, utc(row.created_at))
    opened, intervals = {}, []
    for row in rows:
        if row.action == 'batch_window_restart':
            continue
        # Existing release drains use prefix:pause/resume; new maintenance
        # records carry an explicit incident. Scope is part of the identity.
        payload = json.loads(row.payload_json or '{}')
        incident = (row.event_key.rsplit(':', 1)[0] if row.action.startswith('release_admission_')
                    else payload['incident'])
        key = (row.subject_type, row.subject_id, incident)
        point = utc(row.created_at)
        if row.action.endswith('pause'):
            opened.setdefault(key, point)
        elif key in opened:
            intervals.append((max(start, opened.pop(key)), min(end, point)))
    intervals.extend((max(start, point), end) for point in opened.values())
    covered, cursor = 0.0, start
    for left, right in sorted(intervals):
        left = max(left, cursor)
        if right > left:
            covered += (right-left).total_seconds()
            cursor = right
    return start + timedelta(seconds=covered)


def record_operator_retry(db, *, run_id, actor, step_attempt_ids, now=None):
    """人工 checkpoint 重试 = 给这条 run 开新一代传输预算，并重置 24 小时截止时钟。

    冻结的兜底策略按账本历史数「3 次批量机会 + 24 小时截止」。2026-09-18 实证：
    09-17 的 270 条死信人工重试后 37/40 在 10 秒内、没发一次模型调用就再次判
    ``model_transport_attempts_exhausted``——历史已经记满，重试只是换了个步骤尝试号。
    运维点「重试」的含义是「我认了可能重复计费，再给它一轮」，所以这里落一条
    ``batch_window_restart``：``effective_queue_time`` 以它重置时钟，
    ``_transport_context`` 只数它之后的账本行。旧账本行一律保留，费用与身份不改。
    """
    if type(run_id) is not int or run_id <= 0:
        raise ValueError('invalid run_id')
    ordinal = 1 + int(db.scalar(select(func.count(AuditEvent.id)).where(
        AuditEvent.subject_type == 'production_run', AuditEvent.subject_id == str(run_id),
        AuditEvent.action == 'batch_window_restart')) or 0)
    key = f'batch-clock:production_run:{run_id}:operator-retry:{ordinal}'
    return append_audit_event(db, category='runtime_clock', action='batch_window_restart',
        subject_type='production_run', subject_id=str(run_id), actor=actor,
        payload={'incident': f'operator-retry:{ordinal}', 'step_attempt_ids': sorted(int(i) for i in step_attempt_ids)},
        event_key=key, created_at=utc(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)))


def latest_batch_window_restart(db, *, run_id):
    """最近一次人工重开传输窗口的时间；没有则 None。"""
    value = db.scalar(select(func.max(AuditEvent.created_at)).where(
        AuditEvent.subject_type == 'production_run', AuditEvent.subject_id == str(run_id),
        AuditEvent.action == 'batch_window_restart'))
    return utc(value) if value is not None else None


def rows_after_restart(rows, restart_at):
    """新一代只数窗口重开之后的账本行；``restart_at`` 为 None 时原样返回。"""
    if restart_at is None:
        return list(rows)
    boundary = utc(restart_at)
    return [row for row in rows if utc(row.created_at) > boundary]


def record_maintenance(db, *, incident, paused, actor, run_id=None, now=None):
    """Deployment-only operation; caller owns the admission/maintenance gate."""
    if not isinstance(incident, str) or not 1 <= len(incident) <= 100:
        raise ValueError('invalid maintenance incident')
    subject, identity = ('production_run', str(run_id)) if run_id is not None else ('runtime', 'all')
    action = 'maintenance_pause' if paused else 'maintenance_resume'
    key = f'batch-clock:{subject}:{identity}:{incident}:{action}'
    existing = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key == key))
    if existing:
        return existing
    if not paused and not db.scalar(select(AuditEvent.id).where(
            AuditEvent.event_key == key.replace('maintenance_resume', 'maintenance_pause'))):
        raise ValueError('maintenance resume requires matching pause')
    event = append_audit_event(db, category='runtime_clock', action=action,
        subject_type=subject, subject_id=identity, actor=actor, payload={'incident': incident}, event_key=key,
        created_at=utc(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)))
    return event


def restart_batch_window(db, *, run_id, incident, actor, now=None):
    """Explicit, idempotent per-material restart under the run admission lock.

    Scope/approval is checked by the release operation before calling this.
    In-flight and completed materials cannot be reset. Online stickiness is
    determined from physical invocation history, independent of this clock.
    """
    if not isinstance(incident, str) or not 1 <= len(incident) <= 100:
        raise ValueError('invalid window incident')
    run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.id == run_id)
        .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if run is None:
        raise ValueError('material run missing')
    key = f'batch-clock:restart:{run_id}:{incident}'
    existing = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key == key))
    if existing:
        return existing
    if (run.status != 'paused' or db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt.id).where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id,
            ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_(['running', 'leased'])).limit(1))
            or db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation.id).where(
            WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run_id,
            WorkflowModelInvocation.status == 'reserved').limit(1))):
        raise ValueError('window restart requires paused material without unresolved invocation')
    event = append_audit_event(db, category='runtime_clock', action='batch_window_restart',
        subject_type='production_run', subject_id=run_id, actor=actor, event_key=key,
        payload={'incident': incident, 'original_created_at': utc(run.created_at).isoformat(),
                 'snapshot_hash': run.snapshot_hash}, created_at=utc(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)))
    return event

"""Employee API write boundary (service-token APIs do not use current_user).

Unknown writes require admin. This allowlist complements, never removes, each
service's budget, frozen-version, idempotency and publication checks.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from . import models as m
from .authz import effective_role, has_permission, require_categories

# Each value is (capability, database ownership resolver). Exact routed paths
# include compatibility endpoints; no URL-prefix grants to an entire subsystem.
WRITE_POLICIES: dict[tuple[str, str], tuple[str, str]] = {}


def _allow(method: str, paths: list[str], permission: str, resolver: str) -> None:
    for path in paths:
        WRITE_POLICIES[(method, path)] = (permission, resolver)
