def check_origin(request: Request) -> None:
    """Cookie writes cannot originate from another site, including login CSRF."""
    if request.method in {"GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"}:
        return
    if request.headers.get("sec-fetch-site") == "cross-site":
        raise HTTPException(403, "不接受跨站操作")
    source = request.headers.get("origin") or request.headers.get("referer")
    if source:
        supplied, actual = urlsplit(source), urlsplit(str(request.base_url))
        from .dingtalk_identity import identity_settings
        config = identity_settings()
        canonical = urlsplit(config.redirect_uri)
        # A configured HTTPS public origin also works behind the private HTTP
        # gateway; arbitrary forwarded headers never supply this authority.
        if config.ready and actual.netloc == canonical.netloc:
            actual = canonical
        def origin(value):
            try:
                return value.scheme, value.hostname, value.port or (443 if value.scheme == "https" else 80)
            except ValueError:
                return None
        if origin(supplied) is None or origin(supplied) != origin(actual):
            raise HTTPException(403, "操作来源不匹配")


def _row(db: Session, model, identity):
    if identity is None:
        raise HTTPException(422, "缺少业务对象标识")
    # Pin ownership until this business transaction commits. A concurrent
    # administrator category change must order before or after the write.
    row = db.scalar(select(model).where(model.id == identity).with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if row is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "业务对象不存在")
    return row


def _asset_categories(db: Session, ids: list[int]) -> set[str | None]:
    if not ids or len(ids) > 5000 or any(type(i) is not int or i <= 0 for i in ids):
        raise HTTPException(422, "素材范围无效")
    rows = db.execute(select(m.Asset.id, m.Asset.category_key).where(m.Asset.id.in_(ids))
        .order_by(m.Asset.id).with_for_update()).all()
    if len(rows) != len(set(ids)):
        raise HTTPException(404, "包含不存在的素材")
    return {r.category_key for r in rows}


def _evaluation(db: Session, identity) -> set[str | None]:
    row = _row(db, m.EvaluationResult, identity)
    categories = {_row(db, m.Asset, row.asset_id).category_key}
    if row.production_run_id:
        categories.add(_row(db, m.ProductionRun, row.production_run_id).category_key)
    return categories


def object_categories(db: Session, resolver: str, params: dict, body: dict) -> set[str | None]:
    params = {key: int(value) if key.endswith("_id") and str(value).isdigit() else value for key, value in params.items()}
    if resolver in {"cover_run", "cover_item"}:
        from .cover_regression import CoverRegressionRun, CoverRegressionItem
        run = _row(db, CoverRegressionRun, str(params.get("run_id")))
        if resolver == "cover_item":
            item = _row(db, CoverRegressionItem, str(params.get("item_id")))
            if item.run_id != run.id:
                raise HTTPException(404, "样本不属于本轮")
            return {run.category_key} | _asset_categories(db, [item.asset_id])
        return {run.category_key}
    if resolver == "evaluation":
        return _evaluation(db, params.get("evaluation_id"))
    if resolver == "evaluation_batch":
        items = body.get("items")
        if not isinstance(items, list) or not 1 <= len(items) <= 200:
            raise HTTPException(422, "批量对象范围无效")
        result: set[str | None] = set()
        for item in items:
            if not isinstance(item, dict) or type(item.get("evaluation_id")) is not int:
                raise HTTPException(422, "批量对象无效")
        for item in sorted(items, key=lambda item: item["evaluation_id"]):
            result |= _evaluation(db, item.get("evaluation_id"))
        return result
    if resolver in {"baseline_item", "baseline_set_item"}:
        model = m.BaselineRegressionItem if resolver == "baseline_item" else m.BaselineSetItem
        row = _row(db, model, params.get("item_id"))
        parent_key = "run_id" if resolver == "baseline_item" else "baseline_set_id"
        if getattr(row, parent_key) != params.get(parent_key):
            raise HTTPException(404, "条目不属于指定集合")
        parent = _row(db, m.BaselineRegressionRun if resolver == "baseline_item" else m.BaselineSet, getattr(row, parent_key))
        return {parent.category_key} | _asset_categories(db, [row.asset_id])
    if resolver in {"baseline_set", "baseline_run"}:
        parent = _row(db, m.BaselineSet if resolver == "baseline_set" else m.BaselineRegressionRun,
                      params.get("baseline_set_id" if resolver == "baseline_set" else "run_id"))
        model, key = (m.BaselineSetItem, "baseline_set_id") if resolver == "baseline_set" else (m.BaselineRegressionItem, "run_id")
        categories = set(db.scalars(select(m.Asset.category_key).join(model, model.asset_id == m.Asset.id)
            .where(getattr(model, key) == parent.id).order_by(m.Asset.id).with_for_update()).all())
        return categories | {parent.category_key}
    if resolver == "baseline_correction":
        return {_row(db, m.BaselineCorrectionRun, params.get("correction_id")).category_key}
    if resolver == "sample_set":
        row = _row(db, m.SampleSet, body.get("sample_set_id"))
        return {row.category_key} | set(db.scalars(select(m.Asset.category_key).join(m.SampleSetItem)
            .where(m.SampleSetItem.sample_set_id == row.id).order_by(m.Asset.id).with_for_update()).all())
    if resolver in {"prompt_item", "paired_item", "migration_item"}:
        row = _row(db, m.MigrationItem if resolver == "migration_item" else m.PromptRegressionItem, params.get("item_id"))
        if row.run_id != params.get("run_id"):
            raise HTTPException(404, "条目不属于指定运行")
        if resolver == "migration_item":
            return _asset_categories(db, [row.asset_id])
        sample = _row(db, m.SampleSetItem, row.sample_item_id)
        categories = _asset_categories(db, [sample.asset_id]) | {_row(db, m.SampleSet, sample.sample_set_id).category_key}
        if resolver == "paired_item":
            categories |= _evaluation(db, body.get("baseline_evaluation_id")) | _evaluation(db, body.get("candidate_evaluation_id"))
        return categories
    if resolver in {"runtime_id", "runtime_key"}:
        row = (_row(db, m.ProductionRun, params.get("run_id")) if resolver == "runtime_id" else
               db.scalar(select(m.ProductionRun).where(m.ProductionRun.run_key == params.get("run_key"))
                   .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True)))
        if row is None:
            raise HTTPException(404, "运行不存在")
        return {row.category_key}
    if resolver == "material_job":
        from .incremental_material_models import IncrementalMaterialJob
        return {_row(db, IncrementalMaterialJob, params.get("job_id")).category_key}
    if resolver == "assets":
        return _asset_categories(db, body.get("asset_ids", []))
    if resolver == "package":
        row = _row(db, m.MaterialPackage, body.get("material_package_id"))
        return {row.category_key} | set(db.scalars(select(m.Asset.category_key).join(m.MaterialPackageItem)
            .where(m.MaterialPackageItem.package_id == row.id).order_by(m.Asset.id).with_for_update()).all())
    if resolver in {"production_run", "production_evaluation"}:
        row = _row(db, m.EvaluationProductionRun, params.get("run_id"))
        categories = {row.category_key}
        if resolver == "production_evaluation":
            categories |= _evaluation(db, params.get("evaluation_id"))
        return categories
    raise HTTPException(403, "该操作仅系统管理员可执行")


def authorize_employee_write(request: Request, db: Session, user: m.User, body: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    if request.method in {"GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"}:
        return
    check_origin(request)
    if effective_role(user) == "admin":
        return
    path = getattr(request.scope.get("route"), "path", "").rstrip("/")
    policy = WRITE_POLICIES.get((request.method, path))
    if policy is None or not has_permission(user, policy[0]):
        raise HTTPException(403, "当前角色不能执行此操作")
    resolver = policy[1]
    if resolver in {"self", "read"}:
        return
    if resolver == "draft":
        if body.get("status", "draft") not in {"draft", "candidate"} or body.get("active") is True:
            raise HTTPException(403, "产研只能编辑草稿，正式启用由系统管理员执行")
        return
    categories = object_categories(db, resolver, request.path_params, body)
    require_categories(user, categories)
    # A supplied category is never authoritative, but must also agree with the
    # source objects so it cannot redirect a write into another category.
    if body.get("category_key") and body["category_key"] not in categories:
        raise HTTPException(403, "请求类目与实际对象不一致")
    db.info["employee_object_guard"] = (resolver, dict(request.path_params), body)


def recheck_employee_objects(db: Session, user: m.User) -> None:
    guard = db.info.get("employee_object_guard")
    if guard:
        resolver, params, body = guard
        categories = object_categories(db, resolver, params, body)
        require_categories(user, categories)
        if body.get("category_key") and body["category_key"] not in categories:
            raise HTTPException(403, "对象类目已变化，请刷新后重试")

===== FILE backend/app/employee_access_readiness.py =====
"""Read-only, explicit employee-auth cutover gate; no account is auto-promoted."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
import os

from sqlalchemy import select

from .authz import ROLE_PERMISSIONS
from .dingtalk_identity import identity_settings
from .employee_lifecycle import aware
from .models import EmployeeIdentity, User


def stream_connected() -> bool:
    from .config import get_settings
    from .employee_identity_worker import stream_configuration_key
    try:
        status = json.loads((get_settings().data_dir / "runtime" / "employee-stream-status.json").read_text())
        if not isinstance(status, dict):
            return False
        age = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - aware(datetime.fromisoformat(status["checked_at"]))).total_seconds()
        return status.get("connected") is True and status.get("configuration_key") == stream_configuration_key() and 0 <= age <= 60
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError):
        return False


def readiness(db) -> dict:
    config = identity_settings()
    active = list(db.scalars(select(User).where(User.is_active.is_(True))))
    identities = {row.user_id: row for row in db.scalars(select(EmployeeIdentity).where(EmployeeIdentity.user_id.is_not(None)))}
    bound_admins = 0
    unbound = 0
    stale = 0
    unmapped = 0
    for user in active:
        row = identities.get(user.id)
        unmapped += user.role not in ROLE_PERMISSIONS or (user.role == "admin") != bool(user.is_admin)
        valid = bool(row and row.corp_id == config.corp_id and row.status == "active")
        if not valid:
            unbound += 1
            continue
        fresh = bool(row.verified_at and (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - aware(row.verified_at)).total_seconds() < config.freshness_seconds)
        stale += not fresh
        bound_admins += bool(user.is_admin and fresh)
    stream_enabled = os.getenv("DINGTALK_STREAM_ENABLED", "0") == "1"
    connected = stream_connected() if stream_enabled else False
    return {"configured": config.ready, "employee_required": config.required, "stream_enabled": stream_enabled,
        "stream_connected": connected,
        "active_accounts": len(active), "unbound_accounts": unbound, "unmapped_roles": unmapped,
        "stale_memberships": stale, "verified_admins": bound_admins,
        "cutover_ready": bool(config.ready and connected and bound_admins >= 2 and not (unbound or stale or unmapped))}


def validate_configuration() -> None:
    config = identity_settings()
    if config.required and (not config.ready or os.getenv("DINGTALK_STREAM_ENABLED", "0") != "1"):
        raise RuntimeError("Employee enforcement requires HTTPS, configured DingTalk and organization events")


def main() -> None:
    from .database import SessionLocal
    with SessionLocal() as db:
        result = readiness(db)
    print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
    raise SystemExit(0 if result["cutover_ready"] else 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== FILE backend/app/employee_accounts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
from typing import Literal

from fastapi import HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator
from sqlalchemy import delete, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import append_audit_event
from .authz import lock_access_users
from .dingtalk_identity import IdentityUnavailable, NotCompanyEmployee, identity_client, identity_settings
from .employee_lifecycle import aware
from .models import EmployeeIdentity, EvaluationCategoryProfile, SessionToken, User

Role = Literal["admin", "category_manager", "product_rd", "viewer"]


class UserUpdateRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    display_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    role: Role
    is_active: bool = True
    category_keys: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=100)
    employee_identity_id: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)

    @field_validator("reason", "display_name")
    @classmethod
    def not_blank(cls, value: str) -> str:
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("不能为空")
        return value.strip()


class UserCreateRequest(UserUpdateRequest):
    username: str = Field(min_length=2, max_length=80, pattern=r"^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+$")
    role: Role = "viewer"
    employee_identity_id: int = Field(ge=1)


def _categories(db: Session, payload: UserUpdateRequest) -> list[str]:
    requested = sorted(set(payload.category_keys))
    if payload.role != "category_manager":
        if requested:
            raise HTTPException(422, "仅类目管理员需要分配类目")
        return []
    existing = set(db.scalars(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key).where(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key.in_(requested))))
    if existing != set(requested):
        raise HTTPException(422, "包含不存在的评测类目")
    return requested


def _binding(db: Session, identity_id: int, user_id: int | None, *, reopen: bool = False) -> EmployeeIdentity:
    row = db.scalar(select(EmployeeIdentity).where(EmployeeIdentity.id == identity_id).with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if row is None or row.corp_id != identity_settings().corp_id:
        raise HTTPException(422, "请选择已核验的公司员工")
    if row.user_id is not None and row.user_id != user_id:
        raise HTTPException(409, "该员工已绑定其他账号")
    stale = not row.verified_at or (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - aware(row.verified_at)).total_seconds() >= identity_settings().freshness_seconds
    if stale or row.status != "active":
        if row.status == "removed" and not reopen:
            raise HTTPException(403, "离职员工须由管理员明确重新开通")
        try:
            employee = identity_client().employee(row.employee_id)
        except (IdentityUnavailable, NotCompanyEmployee):
            raise HTTPException(503, "无法核实员工当前在职状态") from None
        if employee.corp_id != row.corp_id or employee.union_id != row.union_id:
            raise HTTPException(409, "员工身份不一致")
        row.verified_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if reopen:
            row.status = "active"
            row.reopened_at = row.verified_at
    return row


def _snapshot(user: User) -> dict:
    return {"display_name": user.display_name, "role": user.role, "is_active": user.is_active,
            "is_admin": user.is_admin, "category_keys": json.loads(user.category_keys_json or "[]")}


def create_employee_account(db: Session, payload: UserCreateRequest, actor: User) -> User:
    categories = _categories(db, payload)
    identity = _binding(db, payload.employee_identity_id, None)
    if db.scalar(select(User.id).where(User.username == payload.username)) is not None:
        raise HTTPException(409, "用户名已存在")
    user = User(username=payload.username, display_name=payload.display_name,
        password_hash="!uninitialized", password_initialized=False,
        role=payload.role, is_admin=payload.role == "admin", is_active=payload.is_active,
        category_keys_json=json.dumps(categories), permission_revision=1)
    db.add(user)
    db.flush()
    identity.user_id = user.id
    append_audit_event(db, category="auth", action="user_created", subject_type="user", subject_id=str(user.id),
        actor=actor.username, payload={"actor_user_id": actor.id, "before": None, "after": _snapshot(user),
            "employee_identity_id": identity.id, "reason": payload.reason})
    db.commit()
    return user


def update_employee_account(db: Session, user_id: int, payload: UserUpdateRequest, actor: User) -> User:
    lock_access_users(db, [actor.id, user_id])
    user = db.get(User, user_id)
    if user is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "账号不存在")
    if user.id == actor.id and (not payload.is_active or payload.role != "admin"):
        raise HTTPException(422, "不能停用当前管理员或移除自己的管理员权限")
    if (not payload.is_active or payload.role != "admin") and user.is_admin:
        if (db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(User).where(User.is_active.is_(True), User.is_admin.is_(True))) or 0) <= 1:
            raise HTTPException(422, "至少保留一个启用中的系统管理员")
    current_identity = db.scalar(select(EmployeeIdentity).where(EmployeeIdentity.user_id == user.id))
    before = {**_snapshot(user), "employee_identity_id": current_identity.id if current_identity else None}
    identity = None
    if payload.employee_identity_id:
        if current_identity and current_identity.id != payload.employee_identity_id:
            raise HTTPException(409, "已有身份绑定不能替换，请走账号恢复流程")
        identity = _binding(db, payload.employee_identity_id, user.id, reopen=payload.is_active and not user.is_active)
    elif payload.is_active and current_identity:
        identity = _binding(db, current_identity.id, user.id, reopen=not user.is_active)
    elif payload.is_active and identity_settings().required:
        raise HTTPException(422, "启用账号前必须绑定公司钉钉员工")
    categories = _categories(db, payload)
    user.display_name, user.role, user.is_active = payload.display_name, payload.role, payload.is_active
    user.is_admin = payload.role == "admin"
    user.category_keys_json = json.dumps(categories)
    if identity:
        identity.user_id = user.id
    after = {**_snapshot(user), "employee_identity_id": (identity or current_identity).id if (identity or current_identity) else None}
    changed = any(before[key] != after[key] for key in ("role", "is_admin", "is_active", "category_keys")) or (identity is not None and current_identity is None)
    if changed:
        user.permission_revision += 1
        db.execute(delete(SessionToken).where(SessionToken.user_id == user.id))
    append_audit_event(db, category="auth", action="user_updated", subject_type="user", subject_id=str(user.id),
        actor=actor.username, payload={"actor_user_id": actor.id, "before": before, "after": after,
            "employee_identity_id": identity.id if identity else None, "reason": payload.reason})
    db.commit()
    return user

===== FILE backend/app/employee_auth_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import secrets
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request, Response
from fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy import delete, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import append_audit_event
from .config import get_settings
from .database import get_db
from .dingtalk_identity import IdentityUnavailable, NotCompanyEmployee, identity_client, identity_settings, private_lan_ipv4
from .employee_access import check_origin
from .employee_lifecycle import aware, employee_login_user, verified_identity
from .login_throttle import LoginThrottled, reserve_login_attempt
from .models import EmployeeIdentity, LoginChallenge, SessionToken, User
from .security import hash_password, hash_session_token


def safe_return_path(value: str) -> str:
    if len(value) > 300 or not value.startswith("/") or value.startswith("//") or "\\" in value or any(ord(c) < 32 for c in value):
        return "/"
    parsed = urlsplit(value)
    if parsed.scheme or parsed.netloc or parsed.fragment or not (parsed.path == "/" or parsed.path.startswith("/workflow/") or parsed.path == "/account"):
        return "/"
    return value


def _challenge_cookie() -> str:
    return get_settings().session_cookie_name + "_dingtalk"


def _throttle(db: Session, request: Request) -> None:
    try:
        reserve_login_attempt(db, scope="dingtalk", identity=request.client.host if request.client else "unknown", limit=30, window_seconds=60)
    except LoginThrottled as exc:
        raise HTTPException(429, "登录尝试过于频繁，请稍后重试", headers={"Retry-After": str(exc.retry_after)}) from None


def require_identity_origin(request: Request) -> None:
    config = identity_settings()
    if config.lan_http_enabled:
        # Uvicorn only trusts the configured private nginx peer. Never derive an
        # allowed origin from arbitrary X-Forwarded-* headers or caller input.
        actual = str(request.base_url).rstrip("/")
        if actual != config.lan_http_origin or not request.client or not private_lan_ipv4(request.client.host):
            raise HTTPException(403, "请从指定内网地址发起钉钉登录")


def create_challenge(db: Session, response: Response, request: Request, method: str, return_to: str, *, secure: bool = True) -> str:
    _throttle(db, request)
    state = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    nonce = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    previous = request.cookies.get(_challenge_cookie())
    if previous:
        db.execute(delete(LoginChallenge).where(LoginChallenge.browser_hash == hash_session_token(previous)))
    expired = list(db.scalars(select(LoginChallenge.state_hash).where(LoginChallenge.expires_at < now).limit(100)))
    if expired:
        db.execute(delete(LoginChallenge).where(LoginChallenge.state_hash.in_(expired)))
    db.add(LoginChallenge(state_hash=hash_session_token(state), browser_hash=hash_session_token(nonce),
        method=method, return_to=safe_return_path(return_to), expires_at=now + timedelta(minutes=5)))
    db.commit()
    response.set_cookie(_challenge_cookie(), nonce, max_age=300, httponly=True, secure=secure, samesite="lax", path="/api/auth/dingtalk")
    return state


def consume_challenge(db: Session, request: Request, state: str, method: str) -> str:
    browser_nonce = request.cookies.get(_challenge_cookie(), "")
    if not browser_nonce or not state or len(state) > 200:
        raise HTTPException(400, "登录验证已失效，请重新发起")
    digest = hash_session_token(state)
    row = db.get(LoginChallenge, digest)
    if row is None or row.method != method or row.browser_hash != hash_session_token(browser_nonce) or aware(row.expires_at) <= datetime.now(timezone.utc):
        raise HTTPException(400, "登录验证已失效，请重新发起")
    return_to = row.return_to
    removed = db.execute(delete(LoginChallenge).where(LoginChallenge.state_hash == digest,
        LoginChallenge.browser_hash == hash_session_token(browser_nonce), LoginChallenge.method == method,
        LoginChallenge.expires_at > datetime.now(timezone.utc)).execution_options(synchronize_session=False)).rowcount
    db.commit()
    if removed != 1:
        raise HTTPException(400, "登录验证已使用，请重新发起")
    return return_to


class WorkbenchCode(BaseModel):
    code: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2048)
    state: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)


class OwnPassword(BaseModel):
    password: str = Field(min_length=10, max_length=200)


class EmployeeLookup(BaseModel):
    employee_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=100)


def build_employee_auth_router(require_user, require_admin, complete_login, user_payload):
    router = APIRouter(tags=["employee-auth"])

    def finish(db: Session, employee, response: Response, method: str):
        user = employee_login_user(db, employee)
        return complete_login(db, user, response, auth_method=method, identity_verified_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc), cookie_secure=identity_settings().cookie_secure)

    @router.get("/api/auth/dingtalk/config")
    def config():
        settings = identity_settings()
        return {"enabled": settings.ready, "employee_required": settings.required,
                "client_id": settings.client_id if settings.ready else None,
                "corp_id": settings.corp_id if settings.ready else None}

    @router.get("/api/auth/dingtalk/start")
    def browser_start(request: Request, return_to: str = "/", db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        require_identity_origin(request)
        try:
            client = identity_client()
        except IdentityUnavailable as exc:
            raise HTTPException(503, str(exc)) from None
        response = RedirectResponse("/", status_code=302)
        state = create_challenge(db, response, request, "browser", return_to, secure=identity_settings().cookie_secure)
        response.headers["location"] = client.authorize_url(state)
        response.headers["cache-control"] = "no-store"
        response.headers["referrer-policy"] = "no-referrer"
        return response

    @router.get("/api/auth/dingtalk/callback")
    def browser_callback(request: Request, state: str = "", code: str = "", authCode: str = "", db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        require_identity_origin(request)
        response = RedirectResponse("/login?identity_error=unavailable", status_code=303)
        response.headers["cache-control"] = "no-store"
        response.headers["referrer-policy"] = "no-referrer"
        try:
            target = consume_challenge(db, request, state, "browser")
            login_code = authCode or code
            if not login_code or len(login_code) > 2048:
                raise HTTPException(400, "钉钉未返回有效授权")
            employee = identity_client().browser_code(login_code)
            payload = finish(db, employee, response, "dingtalk_browser")
            response.headers["location"] = "/account" if not payload["password_initialized"] else safe_return_path(target)
        except (IdentityUnavailable, NotCompanyEmployee, HTTPException) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            reason = "disabled" if isinstance(exc, HTTPException) and "停用" in str(exc.detail) else "unavailable"
            response.headers["location"] = "/login?identity_error=" + reason
        response.delete_cookie(_challenge_cookie(), path="/api/auth/dingtalk", secure=identity_settings().cookie_secure, httponly=True, samesite="lax")
        return response

    @router.post("/api/auth/dingtalk/workbench/start")
    def workbench_start(request: Request, response: Response, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        require_identity_origin(request)
        check_origin(request)
        if not identity_settings().ready:
            raise HTTPException(503, "钉钉登录尚未配置完成")
        return {"state": create_challenge(db, response, request, "workbench", "/", secure=identity_settings().cookie_secure)}

    @router.post("/api/auth/dingtalk/workbench")
    def workbench_login(payload: WorkbenchCode, request: Request, response: Response, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        require_identity_origin(request)
        check_origin(request)
        consume_challenge(db, request, payload.state, "workbench")
        try:
            employee = identity_client().workbench_code(payload.code)
            return finish(db, employee, response, "dingtalk_workbench")
        except (IdentityUnavailable, NotCompanyEmployee) as exc:
            raise HTTPException(503 if isinstance(exc, IdentityUnavailable) else 403, str(exc)) from None

    @router.post("/api/auth/password")
    def own_password(payload: OwnPassword, request: Request, response: Response, user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        session = request.state.auth_session
        if not session.identity_verified_at or (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - aware(session.identity_verified_at)).total_seconds() > 600:
            raise HTTPException(403, "请先重新通过钉钉验证身份，再设置密码")
        initialized = user.password_initialized
        user.password_hash = hash_password(payload.password)
        user.password_initialized = True
        user.permission_revision += 1
        db.execute(delete(SessionToken).where(SessionToken.user_id == user.id))
        append_audit_event(db, category="auth", action="password_changed", subject_type="user", subject_id=str(user.id),
            actor=user.username, payload={"actor_user_id": user.id, "before_initialized": initialized, "after_initialized": True, "reason": "本人通过钉钉重新验证后设置"})
        result = complete_login(db, user, response, auth_method="password", identity_verified_at=None, cookie_secure=identity_settings().cookie_secure)
        return result

    @router.get("/api/employees")
    def employees(_actor: User = Depends(require_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        rows = db.scalars(select(EmployeeIdentity).order_by(EmployeeIdentity.display_name, EmployeeIdentity.id)).all()
        return {"items": [{"id": row.id, "display_name": row.display_name, "employee_id": row.employee_id,
            "user_id": row.user_id, "status": row.status, "verified_at": row.verified_at} for row in rows]}

    @router.get("/api/employees/readiness")
    def employee_readiness(_actor: User = Depends(require_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        from .employee_access_readiness import readiness
        return readiness(db)

    @router.post("/api/employees/verify")
    def lookup_employee(payload: EmployeeLookup, _actor: User = Depends(require_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        try:
            employee = identity_client().employee(payload.employee_id)
        except (IdentityUnavailable, NotCompanyEmployee) as exc:
            raise HTTPException(503 if isinstance(exc, IdentityUnavailable) else 403, str(exc)) from None
        row = verified_identity(db, employee)
        db.commit()
        return {"id": row.id, "display_name": row.display_name, "employee_id": row.employee_id, "status": row.status}

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/employee_identity_worker.py =====
"""Outbound-only organization events and bounded compensation verification.

Protocol: https://open-dingtalk.github.io/developerpedia/docs/learn/stream/protocol/
The published Python SDK 0.24.3 logs connection tickets and has unbounded HTTP
requests; this small EVENT/SYSTEM adapter uses bounded I/O and sanitized logs.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import hashlib
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import json
import logging
import os
import threading
import time

from sqlalchemy import select

from .config import get_settings
from .database import SessionLocal
from .dingtalk_identity import identity_client, identity_settings
from .employee_lifecycle import process_identity_event, verified_identity
from .models import EmployeeIdentity, User

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
EVENT_TYPES = {"user_leave_org", "user_add_org", "user_modify_org"}


def consume_event(envelope: dict, sessions=SessionLocal) -> None:
    headers, data = envelope.get("headers"), envelope.get("data")
    if not isinstance(headers, dict) or not isinstance(data, str):
        raise ValueError("invalid organization event")
    body = json.loads(data)
    if not isinstance(body, dict) or headers.get("eventCorpId") != identity_settings().corp_id:
        raise ValueError("invalid organization envelope")
    event_type = headers.get("eventType")
    if event_type not in EVENT_TYPES:
        return
    if body.get("CorpId", headers["eventCorpId"]) != headers["eventCorpId"] or body.get("EventType", event_type) != event_type:
        raise ValueError("conflicting organization envelope")
    born = int(headers["eventBornTime"])
    occurred = datetime.fromtimestamp(born / 1000, timezone.utc)
    if born <= 0 or occurred > datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=5):
        raise ValueError("invalid event time")
    with sessions() as db:
        process_identity_event(db, event_id=headers.get("eventId", ""), corp_id=headers["eventCorpId"],
            event_type=event_type, occurred_at=occurred, employee_ids=body.get("userId", []))
        db.commit()  # ACK only after the disable + session revocation commits.


async def handle_envelope(envelope: dict, sessions=SessionLocal) -> tuple[dict, bool]:
    headers = envelope.get("headers", {})
    if not isinstance(headers, dict) or not isinstance(headers.get("messageId"), str):
        raise ValueError("missing message identity")
    response = {"code": 200, "headers": {"messageId": headers["messageId"], "contentType": "application/json"}, "message": "OK"}
    if envelope.get("type") == "EVENT":
        try:
            await asyncio.to_thread(consume_event, envelope, sessions)
            data = {"status": "SUCCESS", "message": "committed"}
        except Exception:
            # Never log the received body, account identifiers or credentials.
            logger.warning("Organization event not committed; provider retry requested")
            data = {"status": "LATER", "message": "retry"}
        response["data"] = json.dumps(data)
        return response, False
    if envelope.get("type") == "SYSTEM" and headers.get("topic") in {"ping", "disconnect"}:
        response["data"] = envelope.get("data", "{}")
        return response, headers["topic"] == "disconnect"
    raise ValueError("unsupported stream message")


def reconcile_members(stop: threading.Event, sessions=SessionLocal, client=None) -> dict:
    provider = client or identity_client()
    counts = {"checked": 0, "verified": 0, "unavailable": 0}
    after = 0
    while not stop.is_set():
        with sessions() as db:
            rows = db.execute(select(EmployeeIdentity.id, EmployeeIdentity.employee_id).join(User, User.id == EmployeeIdentity.user_id)
                .where(EmployeeIdentity.id > after, EmployeeIdentity.corp_id == provider.config.corp_id,
                    EmployeeIdentity.status == "active", User.is_active.is_(True))
                .order_by(EmployeeIdentity.id).limit(100)).all()
        if not rows:
            break
        for row in rows:
            if stop.is_set():
                break
            after = row.id
            counts["checked"] += 1
            try:
                employee = provider.employee(row.employee_id)
                with sessions() as db:
                    verified_identity(db, employee)
                    db.commit()
                counts["verified"] += 1
            except Exception:
                # Absence, changed visibility or a provider failure is not an
                # authoritative departure event. Stale access is denied separately.
                counts["unavailable"] += 1
            stop.wait(0.1)
    return counts


def _write_status(values: dict) -> None:
    path = get_settings().data_dir / "runtime" / "employee-identity-status.json"
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    staged = path.with_suffix(".tmp")
    staged.write_text(json.dumps({"checked_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), **values}))
    staged.replace(path)


def stream_configuration_key() -> str:
    config = identity_settings()
    return hashlib.sha256((config.corp_id + ":" + config.client_id + ":" + config.redirect_uri).encode()).hexdigest()


def _stream_status(connected: bool) -> None:
    path = get_settings().data_dir / "runtime" / "employee-stream-status.json"
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    staged = path.with_suffix(".tmp")
    staged.write_text(json.dumps({"connected": connected, "checked_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "configuration_key": stream_configuration_key()}))
    staged.replace(path)


def _reconciliation_loop(stop: threading.Event) -> None:
    while not stop.is_set():
        try:
            _write_status(reconcile_members(stop))
        except Exception:
            logger.warning("Organization reconciliation temporarily unavailable")
        stop.wait(240)


async def _stream_loop(stop: threading.Event) -> None:
    from websockets.asyncio.client import connect
    attempt = 0
    while not stop.is_set():
        try:
            uri = await asyncio.to_thread(identity_client().stream_connection)
            async with connect(uri, open_timeout=10, close_timeout=3, ping_interval=20, ping_timeout=20,
                               max_size=1024 * 1024, max_queue=16) as socket:
                attempt = 0
                _stream_status(True)
                last_status = time.monotonic()
                while not stop.is_set():
                    if time.monotonic() - last_status >= 15:
                        _stream_status(True)
                        last_status = time.monotonic()
                    try:
                        raw = await asyncio.wait_for(socket.recv(), timeout=1)
                    except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                        continue
                    response, disconnect = await handle_envelope(json.loads(raw))
                    await socket.send(json.dumps(response))
                    if disconnect:
                        break
        except Exception:
            logger.warning("Organization stream disconnected; reconnect scheduled")
        finally:
            _stream_status(False)
        attempt += 1
        await asyncio.to_thread(stop.wait, min(30, 2 ** min(attempt, 5)))


@contextmanager
def identity_sync(enabled: bool):
    config = identity_settings()
    if not enabled or not config.ready:
        yield
        return
    stop = threading.Event()
    threads = [threading.Thread(target=_reconciliation_loop, args=(stop,), name="employee-reconciliation", daemon=True)]
    if os.getenv("DINGTALK_STREAM_ENABLED", "0") == "1":
        threads.append(threading.Thread(target=lambda: asyncio.run(_stream_loop(stop)), name="employee-stream", daemon=True))
    # The main worker owns process shutdown. No organization work is started in
    # API/executor processes, and no business intake or model slots are changed.
    for thread in threads:
        thread.start()
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        stop.set()
        for thread in threads:
            thread.join(timeout=30)

===== FILE backend/app/employee_lifecycle.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib

from fastapi import HTTPException
from sqlalchemy import delete, select
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import append_audit_event
from .authz import lock_access_users
from .dingtalk_identity import IdentityUnavailable, NotCompanyEmployee, VerifiedEmployee, identity_client, identity_settings
from .models import EmployeeIdentity, IdentityEvent, SessionToken, User


_BINDING_CHANGED = "员工绑定刚刚变更，请重试"


def aware(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    return value if value.tzinfo else value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def verified_identity(db: Session, employee: VerifiedEmployee) -> EmployeeIdentity:
    if employee.corp_id != identity_settings().corp_id:
        raise HTTPException(403, "仅限本公司员工登录")
    row = db.scalar(select(EmployeeIdentity).where(EmployeeIdentity.corp_id == employee.corp_id,
        EmployeeIdentity.employee_id == employee.employee_id))
    if row is None:
        row = EmployeeIdentity(corp_id=employee.corp_id, employee_id=employee.employee_id,
            union_id=employee.union_id, display_name=employee.display_name, status="active")
        db.add(row)
    else:
        expected_user_id = row.user_id
        if expected_user_id:
            lock_access_users(db, [expected_user_id])
        row = db.scalar(select(EmployeeIdentity).where(EmployeeIdentity.id == row.id)
                        .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
        if row.user_id != expected_user_id:
            raise HTTPException(409, _BINDING_CHANGED)
        if row.union_id != employee.union_id:
            raise HTTPException(409, "员工身份绑定冲突，请联系系统管理员")
        if row.status == "removed":
            # Successful re-login/reconciliation never revives a departed account.
            raise HTTPException(403, "账号已停用，重新入职请联系系统管理员开通")
    row.display_name = employee.display_name
    row.verified_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    db.flush()
    return row


def employee_login_user(db: Session, employee: VerifiedEmployee) -> User:
    """First verified company login creates only a reader, in the login transaction.

    Existing bindings/disabled accounts are authoritative. Never match accounts
    by name, phone or username. A competing first login may win the unique
    identity insert; retry once in a fresh transaction and use its binding.
    """
    for attempt in range(2):
        try:
            identity = verified_identity(db, employee)
            if identity.status != "active":
                raise HTTPException(403, "账号已停用，请联系系统管理员")
            if identity.user_id is not None:
                user = db.get(User, identity.user_id)
                if user is None or not user.is_active:
                    raise HTTPException(403, "账号已停用，请联系系统管理员")
                return user
            # Stable, opaque account name; a collision fails closed instead of
            # attaching this identity to an unrelated pre-existing account.
            key = hashlib.sha256((employee.corp_id + "\0" + employee.employee_id).encode()).hexdigest()[:32]
            user = User(username="dd_" + key, display_name=employee.display_name[:80],
                password_hash="!uninitialized", password_initialized=False,
                is_active=True, is_admin=False, role="viewer", permissions_json="[]",
                category_keys_json="[]", permission_revision=1)
            db.add(user)
            db.flush()
            identity.user_id = user.id
            append_audit_event(db, category="auth", action="employee_self_registered",
                subject_type="user", subject_id=str(user.id), actor="dingtalk",
                event_key=f"employee-self-register:{identity.id}",
                payload={"employee_identity_id": identity.id, "reason": "公司员工首次钉钉登录",
                    "after": {"role": "viewer", "is_admin": False, "is_active": True,
                              "password_initialized": False, "category_keys": []}})
            return user
        except IntegrityError:
            db.rollback()
            if attempt:
                raise HTTPException(409, "员工身份或账号冲突，请联系系统管理员") from None
        except HTTPException as exc:
            if exc.status_code != 409 or exc.detail != _BINDING_CHANGED:
                raise
            db.rollback()
            if attempt:
                raise
    raise AssertionError("bounded registration retry exhausted")


def check_employee_access(db: Session, user: User) -> None:
    config = identity_settings()
    row = db.scalar(select(EmployeeIdentity).where(EmployeeIdentity.user_id == user.id))
    if row is None:
        if config.required:
            raise HTTPException(403, "账号尚未绑定公司钉钉身份，请联系系统管理员")
        return  # Explicit migration period only; cutover requires two bound admins.
    if row.status != "active":
        raise HTTPException(401, "员工账号已停用")
    if not config.corp_id or row.corp_id != config.corp_id:
        raise HTTPException(503, "公司身份配置不匹配，请联系系统管理员")
    if row.verified_at and (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - aware(row.verified_at)).total_seconds() < config.freshness_seconds:
        return
    try:
        employee = identity_client().employee(row.employee_id)
    except (IdentityUnavailable, NotCompanyEmployee):
        raise HTTPException(503, "公司身份验证暂不可用，请稍后重试") from None
    verified_identity(db, employee)
    # Persist freshness before the request starts its protected write transaction.
    db.commit()


def process_identity_event(db: Session, *, event_id: str, corp_id: str, event_type: str,
                           occurred_at: datetime, employee_ids: list[str]) -> bool:
    if corp_id != identity_settings().corp_id or not event_id or len(event_id) > 160:
        raise ValueError("identity event envelope invalid")
    if event_type not in {"user_leave_org", "user_add_org", "user_modify_org"}:
        return False
    if not employee_ids or len(employee_ids) > 1000 or any(not isinstance(v, str) or not v or len(v) > 100 for v in employee_ids):
        raise ValueError("identity event members invalid")
    bindings = dict(db.execute(select(EmployeeIdentity.id, EmployeeIdentity.user_id).where(EmployeeIdentity.corp_id == corp_id,
        EmployeeIdentity.employee_id.in_(employee_ids))).all())
    # Same global ordering as user management; no actor/target deadlock cycle.
    lock_access_users(db, [user_id for user_id in bindings.values() if user_id])
    if db.get(IdentityEvent, event_id):
        return False
    rows = db.scalars(select(EmployeeIdentity).where(EmployeeIdentity.corp_id == corp_id,
        EmployeeIdentity.employee_id.in_(employee_ids)).order_by(EmployeeIdentity.id).with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)).all()
    if any(row.id not in bindings or row.user_id != bindings[row.id] for row in rows):
        # A concurrent binding won before identity-row locking. Retry the event
        # with the complete actor set, never take locks in the reverse order.
        raise HTTPException(409, "员工绑定刚刚变更，请重试")
    for row in rows:
        if event_type == "user_leave_org" and row.reopened_at and aware(row.reopened_at) >= aware(occurred_at):
            continue
        if event_type != "user_leave_org" and row.event_at and aware(row.event_at) > aware(occurred_at):
            continue
        # Equal timestamps: removal wins. No event grants or restores access.
        if row.event_at is None or aware(occurred_at) > aware(row.event_at):
            row.event_at = occurred_at
        row.verified_at = None
        if event_type == "user_leave_org":
            row.status = "removed"
            if row.user_id:
                user = db.get(User, row.user_id)
                before = {"is_active": user.is_active, "permission_revision": user.permission_revision}
                user.is_active = False
                user.permission_revision += 1
                db.execute(delete(SessionToken).where(SessionToken.user_id == user.id))
                append_audit_event(db, category="auth", action="employee_removed", subject_type="user",
                    subject_id=str(user.id), actor="dingtalk", event_key=f"identity:{event_id}:{user.id}",
                    payload={"before": before, "after": {"is_active": False, "permission_revision": user.permission_revision},
                             "reason": "员工离职或移出组织", "source_event_id": event_id})
    db.add(IdentityEvent(event_id=event_id, corp_id=corp_id, event_type=event_type, occurred_at=occurred_at))
    db.flush()
    return True

===== FILE backend/app/evaluation_component_catalog.py =====
"""Registered rule-module types and their authoritative content validators.

The catalog is pure and side-effect free.  Frontends consume its business names
and schemas; component-version services consume the same validation entrypoint.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Callable, Mapping

from .category_evaluation_contract import (
    COMMON_MODIFIERS_FORMAT_VERSION,
    COMMON_MODIFIERS_V2_FORMAT_VERSION,
    validate_common_modifiers,
    validate_track_classification,
)
from .dimension_composition import validate_subcategory_dimensions
from .dimension_schema_registry import canonical_json
from .inspiration_anchor_mechanism import (
    ANCHOR_MECHANISM_KEY,
    validate_anchor_mechanism,
)
from .inspiration_quality_rules import (
    CONTRACT_BLOCK_KEY as QUALITY_RULES_KEY,
    validate_quality_rules_block,
)
from .level_scale import resolve_level_scale
from .redline_policy import REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION, validate_redline_policy


class EvaluationComponentError(ValueError):
    """A stable, operator-readable component catalog or content error."""

    def __init__(
        self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 400
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModuleTypeDefinition:
    key: str
    display_name: str
    description: str
    input_schema: Mapping[str, Any]
    output_schema: Mapping[str, Any]
    required_capabilities: tuple[str, ...]
    allows_early_termination: bool
    merge_strategies: tuple[str, ...]
    editor_schema_version: str
    runtime_modes: tuple[str, ...]


MODULE_TYPE_KEYS = (
    "eligibility_redline",
    "track_routing",
    "media_adjustment",
    "quality_cap_exception",
    "dimension_scoring",
    "anchor_calibration",
    "score_level_matcher",
)

# 可选模块类型：在目录里可被创建/编辑/冻结，但不是每个类目的原生机制必备切片
# （evaluation_mechanism_migration / baseline_regression 只对 MODULE_TYPE_KEYS 做完整性校验）。
OPTIONAL_MODULE_TYPE_KEYS = ("dictionary_filter", "classification_level_map", "upstream_type_short_circuit")


def _object_schema(*required: str, properties: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "required": list(required),
        "properties": dict(properties),
        "additionalProperties": True,
    }
